<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I see the current `implicit_router_sft.py` script. It imports `Trainer` from transformers (standard HF Trainer), not a custom WeightedTrainer. The imports show:
- `from transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM, TrainingArguments, Trainer`
- No `WeightedTrainer` import

But according to D032, the original ablation-b was trained with a WeightedTrainer (custom compute_loss). The current script uses standard HF Trainer. So the replications used the CURRENT script (standard HF Trainer), NOT the original one.


The replications all show non-degenerate behavior (internal_recall > 50%), but v5 with ga=1 failed at 26.7%, while the replications used ga=8. So the gradient accumulation setting seems to be what's preventing degeneration, not the WeightedTrainer itself. I need to dig into whether WeightedTrainer actually exists in the codebase and revisit the original ablation-b specifications.

Looking at the registry command, it explicitly sets `--gradient_accumulation_steps 8`, but the saved train_config.json doesn't include this parameter—likely because the script was modified after training or the config saving doesn't capture it. Though checking the replication configs, they do seem to have the gradient accumulation steps recorded.

Since I don't have git access on the server, I'll need to grep for WeightedTrainer to understand when it was removed from the script. The core issue is whether the replications used the same training approach as the original ablation-b run, and despite using standard HF Trainer instead of WeightedTrainer, they still achieved non-degenerate routing with internal recall above 50% across all three seeds.

Now I'm compiling a comparison table to examine the relationship between the trainer type, gradient accumulation steps, and the resulting internal recall scores across these different runs.

Looking at the data, I notice that `gen-v1` used ga=8 but still failed, which is interesting because it used a different script (`generative_router_sft.py`) compared to the original. The replications all used `implicit_router_sft.py` with ga=8 and succeeded consistently. So it seems the script choice is actually the critical factor here—`implicit_router_sft.py` with ga=8 works reliably, while `implicit_router_sft.py` with ga=1 fails, and the different generative script fails regardless of ga settings.

I should verify whether the replications used consistent evaluation methodology and whether they were properly registered in the system, since those details could affect how I interpret the results.
```

D006\\\\n- id: D008\\\\n  date: 2026-05-13 17:09 UTC\\\\n  summary: 1-step labeling 失败 (tie rate 98.2%), plan-001b 必须改为 episode-terminal counterfactual\\\\n    labeling\\\\n  rationale: 'MVP tie-rate 分析: 2880/2894 步 (98.2%) 两种模式 1-step score delta = 0 (稀疏\\\\n    reward)。非 tie 步几乎全是 catastrophic gap (death penalty)。1-step oracle labeling 下\\\\n    router 只能学到\\\\\\\"避死\\\\\\\"——等价于 always-det on death-risk tasks。必须改为 episode-terminal counterfactual\\\\n    labeling: 每步 fork 环境，两条模式各自继续到 episode 结束，以 final episode score 差异作为 label。Phase\\\\n    0 已验证 forking 可行 (p95 latency 2.37s)。GPU 成本显著增加——需要 Agent 估算 action divergence\\\\n    rate 和实际工时。plan-001a (pre-screening) 和 plan-007 (boil rerun) 不受影响，继续运行。'\\\\n  related_experiments:\\\\n  - exp_id: mvp-p1-gpu0-vllm\\\\n    role: source\\\\n    result: tie rate data from episodes.jsonl\\\\n  depends_on:\\\\n  - D007\\\\n- id: D009\\\\n  date: 2026-05-13 17:15 UTC\\\\n  summary: 'Counterfactual labeling 分 pilot + 全量两阶段执行。Pilot: 3 tasks × 10 ep × K=10\\\\n    on GPU1。Non-tie rate > 30% 则全量 K=10，< 15% 则 pivot。'\\\\n  rationale: Action divergence rate 73.1% 确认 per-step routing 有足够 action space，但 1-step\\\\n    tie rate 98.2% 排除了 1-step labeling。Episode-terminal counterfactual labeling (fork\\\\n    at diverge + K-step continuation) 是必要方案。Full K=20 成本 78h 双卡太高。Pilot 先验证 K=10 的\\\\n    label quality (non-tie rate)，再决定全量策略。Pilot 选 melt/change-state/mix-paint（MVP 确认有信号），10\\\\n    ep each（~7-8h 单卡）。如果 K=10 non-tie rate < 15%，per-step routing 可能是 wrong granularity，需要\\\\n    pivot。\\\\n  related_experiments: []\\\\n  depends_on:\\\\n  - C2 重心从 router 转向 labeling method：episode-level counterfactual labeling 绕开 per-step\\\\n    oracle circularity，本身是 methodological contribution\\\\n\\\\n    - 这样 plan-011 威胁降低：论文贡献不在于\\\\\\\"router 比 keywords 好\\\\\\\"，而在于\\\\\\\"counterfactual analysis 揭示\\\\n    death avoidance 机制 + 诊断框架\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n    评估：Reviewer 的结构性论证正确。Episode-level labels 确实无法教 step-level discrimination（D008\\\\n    98.2% tie rate → Plan C → 但 Plan C 的标注粒度太粗）。这是一个独立于预筛计数的结构性限制。\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n    决策：framing pivot 近确定执行。仍等预筛完成获取完整数据（论文需要），但 paper_framing.md 立即更新方向。实验计划不变——router\\\\n    SFT 仍做（负面结果有论文价值），plan-005/011/012 优先级提升。'\\\\n  related_experiments:\\\\n  - - v5 also used the same script with WeightedTrainer, but with ga=1 per its hypothesis, whereas the replications are using ga=8 with the same weighted training approach.

So the key difference appears to be gradient accumulation—v5 failed with ga=1, but all the replications succeed with ga=8. However, gen-v1 also used ga=8 with a different script entirely and still failed, so I need to look at the full comparison across all models to understand what's actually driving success versus failure.

Generating comparison table... So v5 definitely used the same script, and the key question is whether it had WeightedTrainer at that point. Given that the current version has it and v5 was trained before the replications, it likely did. The note about the script being restructured between ablation-b and v5 might refer to other changes, but the core WeightedTrainer functionality was probably already there. Let me focus on what I can actually verify from the replication results—all three runs used the same configuration with ga=8, upsample_minority enabled, batch size of 4, and varying seeds, which should give me a clear picture of the actual performance.

Generating markdown table...

Now I'm looking at the performance metrics across these different seed runs and noticing some concerning patterns. The original seed 42 shows balanced accuracy around 59%, but when I switch to seeds 123, 456, and 789, the internal recall jumps dramatically while accuracy and detection recall plummet—especially with seeds 456 and 789 where accuracy drops below 45%. This suggests the replications aren't actually reproducing the original results, which is a major red flag. I also need to flag that these three experiments have no registry entries, making them impossible to trace back, and the internal recall variance across seeds is suspiciously high.

Looking deeper at what's happening with the model's predictions: as the seed increases, the model predicts more and more samples as internal—seed 42 predicts 107 out of 306 as internal, but by seed 789 it's predicting 205 samples as internal, which is 67% of the dataset. Yet the actual internal samples are only 33 out of 306, or about 11%. The model seems to be learning something fundamentally wrong about what constitutes an internal sample.

The ga=8 parameter appears to be critical for avoiding degenerate solutions based on comparing the failed v5 run with ga=1 against these replications with ga=8, but this doesn't fully explain why gen-v1 also used ga=8 yet still failed.

I'm also noticing there's no logit-level evaluation comparing these models, so I can't confirm whether they have the same zero logit-generation gap issue. More concerning is that the actual accuracy drops significantly as internal recall increases—seed 789 achieves only 39.87% overall accuracy compared to the original ablation-b's 65.36%, which suggests that over-predicting internal samples could actually perform worse than always defaulting to deterministic routing in a real end-to-end system.

For the original ablation-b's gradient accumulation value, the training config doesn't store it explicitly, but the registry spec command line shows `--gradient_accumulation_steps 8`, so using ga=8 for the replication was the correct match. Now I need to check the decisions.yaml file for D030-D032 to see what conclusions were drawn about this replication.

D003\\\\n- id: D005\\\\n  date: 2026-05-13 15:25 UTC\\\\n  summary: MVP Phase 1 判定 PASS — det > internal 在 ≥2/5 task types 上统计显著，oracle > det\\\\n    信号支持 router 训练\\\\n  rationale: melt (+40.15, CI [25, 60.15]) 和 change-state (+40.2, CI [25, 60.15])\\\\n    满足 ≥2/5 Pass 标准。mix-paint (+35.5) 大概率也通过。grow-plant (0.0) 符合预期（pre-registered\\\\n    weak/对照）。最关键发现：oracle > det（melt +16.7, change-state +14.95, mix-paint +10.0）证明\\\\n    selective routing 有额外增量——这是 SimGate router 训练的信号来源。信号机制是 death avoidance（避免 -100\\\\n    致死动作）而非正向引导，论文 framing 需定位为 safety-aware routing。待确认：melt/change-state 数据重复性、mix-paint\\\\n    CI、boil v3 重跑。\\\\n  related_experiments:\\\\n  - D005\\\\n- id: D007\\\\n  date: 2026-05-13 17:01 UTC\\\\n  summary: 'Dream 审稿结果处理: best 4/6, median 3/6, worst 2/6. 三档一致问题修入 framing+plan:\\\\n    tool-use 文献定位, rule-based baseline, death decomposition'\\\\n  rationale: 'Dream 三档一致暴露 3 个结构性问题: (1) 缺 tool-use learning 文献 (Toolformer/ART/Chameleon),\\\\n    核心方法是标准 tool-use SFT pipeline 必须承认并差异化定位; (2) 缺 rule-based routing baseline 无法证明\\\\n    learned signal; (3) ScienceWorld -100 惩罚放大效应未解构。修入 paper_framing.md (tool-use\\\\n    定位段 + 3 新 concerns) 和 experiment_plan.yaml (plan-011 rule-based baseline P0, plan-012\\\\n    death decomposition P0, plan-013 labeling quality P1)。不 re-dream: 改动是补充性 (新 baselines\\\\n    + 定位), 非方向性 pivot。'\\\\n  related_experiments: []\\\\n  depends_on:\\\\n  - D006\\\\n- id: D008\\\\n  date: 2026-05-13 17:09 UTC\\\\n  summary: 1-step labeling 失败 (tie rate 98.2%), plan-001b 必须改为 episode-terminal counterfactual\\\\n    labeling\\\\n  rationale: 'MVP tie-rate 分析: 2880/2894 步 (98.2%) 两种模式 1-step score delta = 0 (稀疏\\\\n    reward)。非 tie 步几乎全是 catastrophic gap (death penalty)。1-step oracle labeling 下\\\\n    router 只能学到\\\\\\\"避死\\\\\\\"——等价于 always-det on death-risk tasks。必须改为 episode-terminal counterfactual\\\\n    labeling: 每步 fork 环境，两条模式各自继续到 episode 结束，以 final episode score 差异作为 label。Phase\\\\n    0 已验证 forking 可行 (p95 latency 2.37s)。GPU 成本显著增加——需要 Agent 估算 action divergence\\\\n    rate 和实际工时。plan-001a (pre-screening) 和 plan-007 (boil rerun) 不受影响，继续运行。'\\\\n  related_experiments:\\\\n  - exp_id: mvp-p1-gpu0-vllm\\\\n    role: source\\\\n    result: tie rate data from episodes.jsonl\\\\n  depends_on:\\\\n  - D007\\\\n- id: D009\\\\n  date: 2026-05-13 17:15 UTC\\\\n  summary: 'Counterfactual labeling 分 pilot + 全量两阶段执行。Pilot: 3 tasks × 10 ep × K=10\\\\n    on GPU1。Non-tie rate > 30% 则全量 K=10，< 15% 则 pivot。'\\\\n  rationale: Action divergence rate 73.1% 确认 per-step routing 有足够 action space，但 1-step\\\\n    tie rate 98.2% 排除了 1-step labeling。Episode-terminal counterfactual labeling (fork\\\\n    at diverge + K-step continuation) 是必要方案。Full K=20 成本 78h 双卡太高。Pilot 先验证 K=10 的\\\\n    label quality (non-tie rate)，再决定全量策略。Pilot 选 melt/change-state/mix-paint（MVP 确认有信号），10\\\\n    ep each（~7-8h 单卡）。如果 K=10 non-tie rate < 15%，per-step routing 可能是 wrong granularity，需要\\\\n    pivot。\\\\n  related_experiments: []\\\\n  depends_on:\\\\n  - D008\\\\n- id: D010\\\\n  date: 2026-05-13 18:13 UTC\\\\n  summary: C2 重构为 \\\\\\\"failure trajectory predictor\\\\\\\"，paper_framing v3 更新 Plan C 定位\\\\n  rationale: Reviewer 分析 Plan C (episode-level labeling) 对论文 story 的三个影响：(1) Router\\\\n    学到的是 trajectory-level risk prediction 而非 step-level routing，C2 应重构为 \\\\\\\"failure trajectory\\\\n    predictor + diagnostic probe\\\\\\\"；(2) Plan C 完全绕开 Dream worst case 的 oracle circularity\\\\n    攻击（不用 per-step tie-breaking，直接用 episode outcome ground truth）；(3) 不做 class weighting，label\\\\n    不平衡反映真实分布。Story 一致性提升：safety-aware framing ↔ episode-level labeling ↔ trajectory\\\\n    risk prediction 三者天然匹配。C1 \\\\\\\"selective > unconditional\\\\\\\" 的关键指标变为 simulator invocation\\\\n    ratio（用更少的 simulator calls 达到接近 always-det 效果）。\\\\n  related_experiments: []\\\\n  depends_on:\\\\n  - D010\\\\n- id: D011\\\\n  date: 2026-05-13 19:09 UTC\\\\n  summary: Framing 调整：det 优势 = 纯 death avoidance，non-death 时 internal 略优。C1 机制明确为\\\\n    \\\\\\\"safety-efficiency tradeoff\\\\\\\"，router 学习 death-risk detection 而非泛泛路由。Labeling 策略维持\\\\n    episode-level 但加 death-aware 后处理。\\\\n  rationale: 13 tasks (8 prescreening + 5 MVP) 一致显示：no-death Δ(D-I) = -4.5 ~ -0.4，det\\\\n    的全部 30-60 分优势来自 internal 的 41-49% death rate vs det 的 5-12% death rate。这意味着：(1)\\\\n    always-det 不是最优（safe steps 有性能代价），selective routing 有理论基础；(2) router 的核心任务是 death-risk\\\\n    detection；(3) plan-001b episode-level labels 将以 death episodes 为主要信号源，non-death\\\\n    episodes 提供少量 internal labels；(4) plan-012 death decomposition 升为论文核心证据。\\\\n  related_experiments:\\\\n  - exp_id: research-prescreening-gpu0\\\\n    role: evidence\\\\n    result: '8/25 tasks: aggregate no-death Δ = -4.5, I_death=48.6%, D_death=5.4%'\\\\n  - exp_id: mvp-p1-gpu1-v3\\\\n    role: evidence\\\\n    result: '5 tasks: aggregate no-death Δ = -0.4, I_death=41%, D_death=12%'\\\\n  depends_on:\\\\n  - D010\\\\n  - D008\\\\n- id: D012\\\\n  date: 2026-05-13 19:26 UTC\\\\n  summary: 'D012: Label 不平衡应对——设条件触发点，预筛完成后决定是否 pivot framing'\\\\n  rationale: 'Reviewer Loop #2 确认 14 tasks 中仅 1 个 internal-preferred（7%），预计全部 25 tasks\\\\n    中 internal-preferred ≤ 3。93% det 标签 → router SFT 退化为 always-det → C1 \\\\\\\"selective\\\\n    > unconditional\\\\\\\" 不成立风险极高。\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n    条件触发：\\\\n\\\\n    - 若 internal-preferred < 3/25（<12%）→ 执行 framing pivot：C1 从 \\\\\\\"selective > unconditional\\\\\\\"\\\\n    降级为 \\\\\\\"det >> internal, 主要机制是 death avoidance\\\\\\\"；C2 调整为 \\\\\\\"router 学习 death-risk trajectory\\\\n    prediction\\\\\\\"；plan-012 (death decomposition) 升为核心贡献；plan-011 (rule-based baseline)\\\\n    变关键（learned vs heuristic）\\\\n\\\\n    - 若 internal-preferred ≥ 3/25（≥12%）→ 保持现有 framing，plan-002 router SFT 使用 class-weighted\\\\n    loss 应对不平衡\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n    不选的方案：(b) 人为增加 internal episodes → p-hacking 风险；(c) 加 MATH track → 12 天 DDL 下风险太大\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n    另外 Reviewer 指出 genuine-det 分类中 find-living-thing（+0.25）和 freeze（+2.0）在 n=5 下无统计意义，仅\\\\n    find-animal（+4.2）可能有真实信号。这进一步削弱 non-death 信号。'\\\\n  related_experiments:\\\\n  - Class-weighted loss 无法 salvage selective routing——问题不是 class imbalance，而是 episode-level\\\\n    labels 把同一 episode 所有步骤（含安全步骤）标为同一 label，router 学到的是 task-type classifier 而非 step-level\\\\n    risk detector\\\\n\\\\n    2. C2 \\\\\\\"early warning signals\\\\\\\" 脆弱（~30% 有实质），需 plan-005 验证 within-task-type 预测方差\\\\n\\\\n    3. Rule-based baseline 有 ~60% 概率让 learned router novelty 归零\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n    Reviewer 建议进一步 pivot：\\\\n\\\\n    - C1 完全放弃 \\\\\\\"selective > unconditional\\\\\\\"，改为纯经验发现：det >> internal，death avoidance\\\\n    是主要机制\\\\n\\\\n    - C2 重心从 router 转向 labeling method：episode-level counterfactual labeling 绕开 per-step\\\\n    oracle circularity，本身是 methodological contribution\\\\n\\\\n    - 这样 plan-011 威胁降低：论文贡献不在于\\\\\\\"router 比 keywords 好\\\\\\\"，而在于\\\\\\\"counterfactual analysis 揭示\\\\n    death avoidance 机制 + 诊断框架\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n    评估：Reviewer 的结构性论证正确。Episode-level labels 确实无法教 step-level discrimination（D008\\\\n    98.2% tie rate → Plan C → 但 Plan C 的标注粒度太粗）。这是一个独立于预筛计数的结构性限制。\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n    决策：framing pivot 近确定执行。仍等预筛完成获取完整数据（论文需要），但 paper_framing.md 立即更新方向。实验计划不变——router\\\\n    SFT 仍做（负面结果有论文价值），plan-005/011/012 优先级提升。'\\\\n  related_experiments:\\\\n  - D006\\n- id: D008\\n  date: 2026-05-13 17:09 UTC\\n  summary: 1-step labeling 失败 (tie rate 98.2%), plan-001b 必须改为 episode-terminal counterfactual\\n    labeling\\n  rationale: 'MVP tie-rate 分析: 2880/2894 步 (98.2%) 两种模式 1-step score delta = 0 (稀疏\\n    reward)。非 tie 步几乎全是 catastrophic gap (death penalty)。1-step oracle labeling 下\\n    router 只能学到\\\"避死\\\"——等价于 always-det on death-risk tasks。必须改为 episode-terminal counterfactual\\n    labeling: 每步 fork 环境，两条模式各自继续到 episode 结束，以 final episode score 差异作为 label。Phase\\n    0 已验证 forking 可行 (p95 latency 2.37s)。GPU 成本显著增加——需要 Agent 估算 action divergence\\n    rate 和实际工时。plan-001a (pre-screening) 和 plan-007 (boil rerun) 不受影响，继续运行。'\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: mvp-p1-gpu0-vllm\\n    role: source\\n    result: tie rate data from episodes.jsonl\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D007\\n- id: D009\\n  date: 2026-05-13 17:15 UTC\\n  summary: 'Counterfactual labeling 分 pilot + 全量两阶段执行。Pilot: 3 tasks × 10 ep × K=10\\n    on GPU1。Non-tie rate > 30% 则全量 K=10，< 15% 则 pivot。'\\n  rationale: Action divergence rate 73.1% 确认 per-step routing 有足够 action space，但 1-step\\n    tie rate 98.2% 排除了 1-step labeling。Episode-terminal counterfactual labeling (fork\\n    at diverge + K-step continuation) 是必要方案。Full K=20 成本 78h 双卡太高。Pilot 先验证 K=10 的\\n    label quality (non-tie rate)，再决定全量策略。Pilot 选 melt/change-state/mix-paint（MVP 确认有信号），10\\n    ep each（~7-8h 单卡）。如果 K=10 non-tie rate < 15%，per-step routing 可能是 wrong granularity，需要\\n    pivot。\\n  related_experiments: []\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D008\\n- id: D010\\n  date: 2026-05-13 18:13 UTC\\n  summary: C2 重构为 \\\"failure trajectory predictor\\\"，paper_framing v3 更新 Plan C 定位\\n  rationale: Reviewer 分析 Plan C (episode-level labeling) 对论文 story 的三个影响：(1) Router\\n    学到的是 trajectory-level risk prediction 而非 step-level routing，C2 应重构为 \\\"failure trajectory\\n    predictor + diagnostic probe\\\"；(2) Plan C 完全绕开 Dream worst case 的 oracle circularity\\n    攻击（不用 per-step tie-breaking，直接用 episode outcome ground truth）；(3) 不做 class weighting，label\\n    不平衡反映真实分布。Story 一致性提升：safety-aware framing ↔ episode-level labeling ↔ trajectory\\n    risk prediction 三者天然匹配。C1 \\\"selective > unconditional\\\" 的关键指标变为 simulator invocation\\n    ratio（用更少的 simulator calls 达到接近 always-det 效果）。\\n  related_experiments: []\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D010\\n- id: D011\\n  date: 2026-05-13 19:09 UTC\\n  summary: Framing 调整：det 优势 = 纯 death avoidance，non-death 时 internal 略优。C1 机制明确为\\n    \\\"safety-efficiency tradeoff\\\"，router 学习 death-risk detection 而非泛泛路由。Labeling 策略维持\\n    episode-level 但加 death-aware 后处理。\\n  rationale: 13 tasks (8 prescreening + 5 MVP) 一致显示：no-death Δ(D-I) = -4.5 ~ -0.4，det\\n    的全部 30-60 分优势来自 internal 的 41-49% death rate vs det 的 5-12% death rate。这意味着：(1)\\n    always-det 不是最优（safe steps 有性能代价），selective routing 有理论基础；(2) router 的核心任务是 death-risk\\n    detection；(3) plan-001b episode-level labels 将以 death episodes 为主要信号源，non-death\\n    episodes 提供少量 internal labels；(4) plan-012 death decomposition 升为论文核心证据。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - 若 internal-preferred < 3/25（<12%）→ 执行 framing pivot：C1 从 \\\"selective > unconditional\\\"\\n    降级为 \\\"det >> internal, 主要机制是 death avoidance\\\"；C2 调整为 \\\"router 学习 death-risk trajectory\\n    prediction\\\"；plan-012 (death decomposition) 升为核心贡献；plan-011 (rule-based baseline)\\n    变关键（learned vs heuristic）\\n\\n    - 若 internal-preferred ≥ 3/25（≥12%）→ 保持现有 framing，plan-002 router SFT 使用 class-weighted\\n    loss 应对不平衡\\n\\n\\n    不选的方案：(b) 人为增加 internal episodes → p-hacking 风险；(c) 加 MATH track → 12 天 DDL 下风险太大\\n\\n\\n    另外 Reviewer 指出 genuine-det 分类中 find-living-thing（+0.25）和 freeze（+2.0）在 n=5 下无统计意义，仅\\n    find-animal（+4.2）可能有真实信号。这进一步削弱 non-death 信号。'\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - C2 重心从 router 转向 labeling method：episode-level counterfactual labeling 绕开 per-step\\n    oracle circularity，本身是 methodological contribution\\n\\n    - 这样 plan-011 威胁降低：论文贡献不在于\\\"router 比 keywords 好\\\"，而在于\\\"counterfactual analysis 揭示\\n    death avoidance 机制 + 诊断框架\\\"\\n\\n\\n    评估：Reviewer 的结构性论证正确。Episode-level labels 确实无法教 step-level discrimination（D008\\n    98.2% tie rate → Plan C → 但 Plan C 的标注粒度太粗）。这是一个独立于预筛计数的结构性限制。\\n\\n\\n    决策：framing pivot 近确定执行。仍等预筛完成获取完整数据（论文需要），但 paper_framing.md 立即更新方向。实验计划不变——router\\n    SFT 仍做（负面结果有论文价值），plan-005/011/012 优先级提升。'\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - Now I have a comprehensive picture. Let me summarize my key findings for the report:

1. **plan-003-e2e-ablation-b was correctly invalidated** — conclusion changed from "negative" to "failed" at 09:10 UTC. My CRITICAL report was acted on.

2. **No corrected full 245-episode e2e eval has been done yet.** There's only a tiny 3-episode test in plan_003_eval (implicit_router_results.json).

3. **The eval_ablation_b_e2e directory was re-written at 17:01 but still contains the wrong-checkpoint results** — the episode_details show 29/1 det/int votes (consistent with generative-v1 checkpoint), while the correct implicit router shows 17/13 votes for the same episode.
   
4. **The seed variation experiments across ablation-b reveal a concerning pattern** — balanced accuracy hovers around 57-59% regardless of seed, barely above random chance, while internal and detection recall swing wildly in opposite directions, suggesting the model is just adjusting its decision threshold rather than learning meaningful discrimination. These experiments were trained and evaluated but never registered.

5. **There's a mismatch in the ablation-b training configuration** — the saved config is missing the gradient_accumulation_steps parameter that should be there.

Now I'm noticing conflicting claims about what ablation-b actually used: one document says it succeeded with ga=1, another claims it used ga=8, but the registry command clearly specifies ga=8, so there's a real discrepancy between what was documented and what the saved config shows. So D027's claim that ablation-b used ga=1 is incorrect — the spec clearly shows ga=8, and the seed variations all match that. This means v5 actually did differ by using ga=1, not ablation-b. The seed reproductions with ga=8 are valid, showing around 58% balanced accuracy where the router barely discriminates between classes.

I also need to verify whether the GPU process running the corrected e2e eval is still active. The process ID I checked earlier is gone, and while there's some GPU memory in use, it's likely just a lingering CUDA context. I should finalize my report now.

The core findings are that the seed variations expose ablation-b's "51.5% internal recall" as unstable — it swings from 51.5% to 81.8% depending on seed, with balanced accuracy hovering around 58%, which barely beats random guessing. This contradicts the paper's claim that ablation-b is the first non-degenerate router. Meanwhile, the corrected e2e evaluation still hasn't completed, and the existing results appear to be from the wrong checkpoint. There's also conflicting information between D027 and D031 about which ga value ablation-b actually used.

[severity: warning] ablation-b seed 变体揭示 router 判别力接近随机\n\n服务器上发现三个未注册的 seed 变体（ablation-b-seed123/456/789），使用相同配置（ga=8, upsample_minority=true, data/plan-004-implicit/），仅 seed 不同。Generation-level eval 结果：\n\n| Seed | balanced_acc | internal_recall | det_recall |\n|------|-------------|-----------------|------------|\n| 42 (原始) | 59.27% | 51.52% | 67.03% |\n| 123 | 57.36% | 57.58% | 57.14% |\n| 456 | 57.48% | 75.76% | 39.19% |\n| 789 | 58.31% | 81.82% | 34.80% |\n\n**核心发现**：\n- balanced_accuracy 在 57-59% 范围内极其稳定 —— **仅比随机(50%)高 ~8pp**\n- internal_recall 从 51% 到 82% 巨幅波动，完全由 seed 决定\n- 模型的判别能力（balanced_acc）在各 seed 间恒定，变的只是分类阈值/偏置\n\n**对论文的影响**：ablation-b 的 \"internal_recall=51.5%\" 不是 router 的固有属性，而是 seed=42 恰好产生的一个阈值。换 seed 后 internal recall 可以高到 82%（但 det_recall 掉到 35%）。如果论文要声称 \"首个非退化 router\"，需要明确说明 balanced_accuracy 仅 ~58%，并讨论这与随机 baseline（50%）的差距。seed 变体实验必须注册并在论文中报告方差。\n\n### 2. [severity: warning] corrected e2e eval 仍未完成\n\n- plan-003-e2e-ablation-b 已正确标为 `conclusion: failed`（09:10 UTC），原 CRITICAL 已处理 ✓\n- 但完整 245-episode 的正确 e2e eval（使用 checkpoints/plan-004-implicit/final_adapter）仍未注册\n- plan_003_eval 下有一个 3-episode 小规模测试（implicit_router_results.json），score=7.0, death_rate=0，但 n=3 无统计意义\n- eval_ablation_b_e2e 目录在 17:01 被更新，但内容与旧（错误 checkpoint）结果完全一致（episode 0: 29 det / 1 int votes）；对比 implicit router 测试同一 episode 为 17 det / 13 int votes，说明可能仍是错误 checkpoint 的结果\n- GPU 0 有一个已退出的 python 进程（PID 256789），可能是刚完成的 eval run\n\n### 3. [severity: warning] D027 与 D031 关于 ablation-b ga 值的矛盾\n\n- D027 声称 \"ablation-b 成功用 ga=1\"\n- D031 声称 \"ablation-b 用 gradient_accumulation_steps=8\"\n- Registry spec 命令明确是 `--gradient_accumulation_steps 8`\n- ablation-b 的 train_config.json 不包含 ga 字段（可能是脚本当时版本不保存此字段）\n- seed 变体 train_config.json 都有 ga=8\n\nD027 的 \"ga=1\" 声明是错误的。这影响了 v5 失败分析：D031 判断 v5(ga=1) 与 ablation-b(ga=8) 不同是正确的方向，但 D027 的错误描述可能造成混淆。建议更正 D027。\n\n### 建议操作\n1. 注册 seed 变体实验到 registry\n2. 尽快用正确 checkpoint + 正确 router_type 跑 245-ep e2e eval\n3. 论文中报告 ablation-b 的 balanced_accuracy ~58% 和 seed 方差，不要单独强调 internal_recall=51.5%\n4. 更正 D027 中关于 ablation-b ga 值的错误描述"
}

```text
New experiment found: `plan-003-eval-implicit`. This is the correct e2e eval using `checkpoints/plan-004-implicit/final_adapter` (ablation-b's actual checkpoint). But the results show it STILL performs worse than always-det:

- trained router: -12.84 (CI: [-17.52, -8.45])
- always-det: -9.47
- delta: -3.37 (worse than always-det by 3.37 pts)

Wait, but this uses a DIFFERENT eval script: `eval_implicit_router.py` (not `eval_end_to_end.py`). Let me check the details.


Looking at the full metrics, the router's underperformance is clearer now—it's routing to the simulator 94% of the time and only achieving 60% step-level routing accuracy, which suggests the learned routing isn't actually learning to discriminate well between deterministic and internal modes despite 82% episode-level accuracy.

The 16 episodes routed to internal are dragging down the overall score by 3.37 points and increasing the death rate from 16.3% to 19.2%, indicating those internal routing decisions are actively harmful. I've confirmed this is the correct checkpoint from the spec, so ablation-b's router is failing to improve over the always-deterministic baseline. I need to verify the evaluation methodology is consistent across these different scripts and directories. The per-step count (2160 steps) aligns reasonably with the episode count, but the 95-episode routing accuracy sample is puzzling — that should correspond to the non-no-preference episodes from the full 245. Let me consolidate my findings on the correct ablation-b end-to-end evaluation and prepare a comprehensive review covering both the seed replication experiments and this validated assessment.

The implicit routing approach scores 3.37 points worse than the deterministic baseline, with a 94.1% routing similarity but a concerning 19.2% death rate that exceeds the baseline. The ablation-b replication shows critical issues: the three seed experiments lack proper registry registration, internal recall varies wildly across seeds (57.6% to 81.8%), and there's conflicting information about version specifications and the necessity of ga=8 for non-degenerate behavior.

I notice D032's claim about using the standard HF Trainer doesn't match the current script which still has WeightedTrainer, so the accuracy of that report is questionable. The plan-003-e2e-ablation-b entry has been marked as INVALID, which is good since I flagged it as critical. Now I'll send my review.

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have the full picture. Let me compile my review and send it.

Key findings for both the replication AND the new e2e eval:

**plan-003-eval-implicit (正确 ablation-b e2e eval):**
- Uses correct checkpoint: `checkpoints/plan-004-implicit/final_adapter`
- Score: -12.84 (CI: [-17.52, -8.45])
- always-det: -9.47 → **router 比 always-det 差 3.37 分**
- oracle: -6.58 → router 远不及 oracle


- Router heavily favors the deterministic path at 93.5%, but those 16 episodes routed to internal are causing higher mortality rates and dragging down performance compared to always-det, with step-level routing accuracy only reaching 60.2% despite episode-level accuracy of 82.1%.
- The death penalty of -100 makes even a small number of wrong routing decisions extremely costly, and the confidence interval [-17.52, -8.45] suggests the performance difference isn't statistically significant.
- The ablation experiment with 3 seeds using the weighted trainer shows consistent internal recall around 57% without any degenerate cases.
- Internal recall varies wildly across seeds (30+ percentage points), though balanced accuracy stays stable at 57-59%.
- I'm realizing v5 might also use the WeightedTrainer despite the documentation saying it uses standard HF Trainer, since the current implicit_router_sft.py still has WeightedTrainer at those lines.
- If v5 also used WeightedTrainer, then the real difference between v5 and ablation-b comes down to something else entirely.

The key insight is that gradient accumulation at 8 steps versus 1 is the critical factor preventing model degradation. But even with ga=8 preventing the always-det collapse, the router still can't outperform always-det in actual deployment, which aligns with the paper's framing that the router functions as an informative diagnostic tool rather than a practical improvement.
```

  "text": "## 审查报告 — plan-003-eval-implicit + ablation-b 复制实验（3 seeds）\n\n**结论**: WARNING\n\n---\n\n### A. plan-003-eval-implicit（正确 checkpoint 的 e2e eval）\n\n[severity: info] **Checkpoint 正确**。spec 确认使用 `checkpoints/plan-004-implicit/final_adapter`（ablation-b 真正的 adapter），此前 CRITICAL（错误 checkpoint）已修复。plan-003-e2e-ablation-b 已标注 INVALID/failed，处理得当。\n\n[severity: warning] **Router 仍然不如 always-det**。trained router 得分 -12.84（CI [-17.52, -8.45]）vs always-det -9.47，差 3.37 分。虽然 CI 包含 -9.47（不显著），但点估计方向是负的。路由分布 229 det / 16 internal（93.5% det），router 极度保守但仅 16 个 internal 决策就拉低了整体得分。\n\n[severity: warning] **Internal 路由引入额外死亡**。catastrophic_failure_rate=19.2% > always-det 的 16.3%。那 16 个被路由到 internal 的 episodes 中有更高比例导致了 catastrophic failure（-100），少量错误决策的代价远超正确路由的收益。\n\n[severity: info] **Episode routing accuracy 82.1%（78/95 labeled episodes）** 看似不错，但在 death-penalty 环境下，17 个错误路由中的每个 death 带来 -100 分惩罚，导致整体负面效果。这与 C1（death avoidance dominance）完全一致：even 少量 misrouting 到高死亡风险的 internal 路径就足以抵消所有正确路由的增益。\n\n**结论**：ablation-b 是项目中唯一有效的 generation-level router（internal_recall=51.5%，zero logit-generation gap），但 e2e 部署仍不如 always-det。这加强了论文核心叙事：death avoidance 主导 routing signal → router 很难超越简单的 always-det 策略。建议在论文 Table 1 中如实报告此结果（trained-router = -12.84），作为 Section 3.3 的一部分。\n\n---\n\n### B. ablation-b 复制实验（seed 123/456/789）\n\n[severity: warning] **三个复制实验均未在 registry 注册**。只有服务器上的 checkpoint 和 eval JSON 文件，无 registry 条目。违反实验追溯要求。建议补注册。\n\n[severity: warning] **internal_recall 跨 seed 方差极大（30pp+）**，决策边界不稳定：\n\n| Seed | Internal Recall | Balanced Acc | Accuracy | Det Recall | Pred Internal/Total |\n|------|----------------|-------------|---------|-----------|---------------------|\n| 42 (orig) | 51.52% | 59.27% | 65.36% | 67.03% | 107/306 |\n| 123 | 57.58% | 57.36% | 57.19% | 57.14% | 136/306 |\n| 456 | 75.76% | 57.48% | 43.14% | 39.19% | 191/306 |\n| 789 | 81.82% | 58.31% | 39.87% | 34.80% | 205/306 |\n\nbalanced_accuracy 稳定（57-59%），但模型路由偏好高度 seed-dependent。seed789 将 67% 样本预测为 internal（真实 internal 仅 10.8%），overall accuracy 降至 39.9%。如果论文引用 ablation-b internal_recall=51.5% 为\"唯一成功\"，需注明这不稳定——换 seed 可能得到 80%+ 或其他值。\n\n[severity: info] **ga=8 确认为非退化必要条件**。对比：v5(ga=1)→internal_recall=26.7%(失败)；三个复制(ga=8)→全部>50%(非退化)。有效 batch size=32 似乎是 generative SFT 学到 minority class 信号的关键。\n\n[severity: warning] **D032 准确性存疑**。D032 声称\"v5 用标准 HF Trainer，ablation-b 用 WeightedTrainer\"，但当前 `implicit_router_sft.py` 仍包含 `class WeightedTrainer(Trainer):` (L199) 且 `trainer = WeightedTrainer(...)` (L352)。v5 也用此脚本训练。如果 v5 也使用了 WeightedTrainer，则 v5 与 ablation-b 的真正差异仅是 ga（1 vs 8），WeightedTrainer 不是区分因素。建议 Agent 确认 v5 训练时脚本是否真的不含 WeightedTrainer（需 git history），否则应修正 D032。\n\n[severity: warning] **无 logit-level eval 对比**。复制实验只做了 generation-level eval，无法确认新 seed 模型是否也具有 zero logit-generation gap。原始 ablation-b 的 zero gap 是项目中的独特发现，若要引用为 stable property 而非偶然，需要跨 seed 验证。\n\n---\n\n### 综合影响\n\n两组结果共同确认：\n1. **ablation-b 是 generation-level 非退化的（跨 4 个 seed 可复现）**，但路由偏好极不稳定\n2. **即使最好的 router 也不能在 e2e 上超越 always-det**（-12.84 vs -9.47）\n3. **这与 C1 narrative 完全一致**：death avoidance 占 90%+ routing signal → 在 death-dominated 环境中 always-det 已接近最优，router 的 misrouting 代价 > 正确路由收益\n\n论文建议：Table 1 增加 trained-router 行（-12.84），Discussion 简述 router 训练困境（non-degenerate but unstable, e2e negative），作为 death dominance 的推论而非独立负面结果。"

Now I have the full picture. Let me review the ROADMAP update and the new decisions D033-D036.

**D033**: Framing 定稿 Option B — balanced_acc ~58% 接近随机，router 不是贡献点。这个判断合理。但我需要注意一个细节：balanced_acc=58% 是否真的"接近随机"？50% 是随机baseline（二分类），58% 比随机高 16% 相对值（8pp 绝对值）。

在极端不平衡下（10.8% internal），这个表现值得进一步讨论。不过对于论文叙事来说，将 router 定位为 informative diagnostic 而非 practical tool 是正确的。

**D034**: D027 更正 — ga=8 confirmed。正确。

**D035**: e2e negative, 纳入 C1 叙事。与我的审查结论一致。正确。

**D036**: 跨 seed 方差确认。与我的审查结论一致。正确。

现在检查核心支撑点：plan-012 的 death decomposition 数据充分（11 tasks/245 episodes, r=0.9698），方向假设有实验支撑；D032 的 WeightedTrainer 问题列为待办合理，没有遗漏关键 ablation；D033 基于 seed 变体数据做出的 framing 决策有数据支撑；写作期间 GPU 空闲，不存在算力瓶颈。

论文的三个核心主张都有充分支撑——death avoidance 90% 的结果、episode-level counterfactual labeling 的方法论贡献、以及 router 负面结果强化了主叙事，虽然单一 benchmark 和 backbone 是潜在风险但已在 limitations 中承认；检查 AI 偏差时未发现过度乐观，论文坦诚 router 失败，只有轻微的 confirmation bias 风险。

关于 critical path，从 5/14 的数据核实到 5/23 的最终提交，各个阶段的时间安排都是合理的，Agent 能够完成这些任务。

不过我注意到 ROADMAP 中"Router 训练总结"部分遗漏了 D033（framing 定稿），虽然这只是表述上的疏漏，不影响实际的工作内容。

```text
Now I see two things:

1. **Three seed experiments registered** (plan-004-ablation-b-seed123, seed456, seed789) — my warning about missing registry entries has been addressed. Good.

2. **New e2e eval**: `plan-003-e2e-ablation-b-correct` — this uses `eval_end_to_end.py` (not `eval_implicit_router.py`) with the CORRECT checkpoint (`checkpoints/plan-004-implicit/final_adapter`). Results:
   - score: -10.70 (CI: [-15.25, -6.38])
   - baseline always-det: -9.47
   - delta: -1.23 (worse than always-det, but less bad than plan-003-eval-implicit's -3.37)
   - death_rate: 0.1755 (vs always-det 0.163)
   - sim_ratio: 0.9703 (97% det)
   - fallback_rate: 0.7061 (173/245 episodes used fallback)
   - success_rate: 0.2367

So now I have two e2e evaluations of the correct checkpoint running in parallel — one using the implicit router evaluation script and this one using the standard end-to-end script — and they're showing different performance profiles.

The implicit router version scores worse at -12.84 with a 94.1% determinism ratio, while the end-to-end version comes in at -10.70 with 97% determinism, suggesting the end-to-end evaluation is more conservative in its routing decisions. This discrepancy between the two evaluation methods is worth digging into to understand what's driving the performance difference.

The fallback rate of around 70.6% appears consistently across both evaluations, indicating this is a data-dependent behavior rather than something the model controls — roughly 173 out of 245 episodes trigger some kind of default fallback when the router lacks step information. I'm also noticing that plan-004-ablation-b was marked as "negative" when it was previously "success," which suggests someone may have updated the conclusion during a recent change, so I should verify whether that's accurate.

There are a few critical issues I need to flag: the two eval scripts for plan-003 are producing meaningfully different results (a 2.14 point gap), so I need to figure out which one should be used as the source of truth for the main table. The seed experiments have now been properly registered, which resolves an earlier concern. And the status flip for plan-004-ablation-b from success to negative warrants clarification — while the model isn't degenerate, its practical utility is questionable at that performance level.

Looking at the confidence intervals for plan-003-e2e-ablation-b-correct, the range of -15.25 to -6.38 encompasses the always-deterministic baseline at -9.47, so there's no statistically significant difference there. I should send a focused review highlighting these points.
